# Head-end known issue register

Each OPEN row affects one read: the read of the meter on read_date delivered by the named head end.
Remedies: STUCK_REGISTER - the register did not advance and the read is unusable; WH_AS_KWH - the head end wrote Wh into kWh fields, divide both registers by 1000; SWAP_REGISTERS - import and export were exchanged. CLOSED rows are history and carry no remedy.
